				<title><![CDATA[Samsung To Invest USD 1 Billion in AI Infrastructure Company Helix]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-to-invest-usd-1-billion-in-ai-infrastructure-company-helix</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 29 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28195526/Samsung-Corporate-AI-Infrastructure-Company-Helix_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[More Stories]]></category>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics, Samsung C&T, Samsung SDS, Samsung SDI, Samsung Life Insurance and Samsung Fire & Marine Insurance today announced they are investing a combined USD 1 billion in Helix Digital Infrastructure, an AI infrastructure company established by global investment firm KKR. Launched in June 2026, Helix is an AI-enabling infrastructure provider that is designed to […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics, Samsung C&T, Samsung SDS, Samsung SDI, Samsung Life Insurance and Samsung Fire & Marine Insurance today announced they are investing a combined USD 1 billion in Helix Digital Infrastructure, an AI infrastructure company established by global investment firm KKR.</p>



<p>Launched in June 2026, Helix is an AI-enabling infrastructure provider that is designed to deliver integrated solutions across the entire value chain, including hyperscale data center development and operations, power generation (covering both baseload and flexible energy sources), transmission and distribution infrastructure, and fiber-optic networks.</p>



<p>Through Helix, hyperscalers can rapidly secure the large-scale, comprehensive infrastructure required to keep pace with exponentially growing AI demands.</p>



<p>Helix is led by Adam Selipsky, former CEO of Amazon Web Services (AWS) and is assembling a dedicated management team comprising leading experts from the data center and power industries. Helix also leverages the capabilities of KKR’s leading global infrastructure business with about 170 dedicated professionals.</p>



<p>Alongside Samsung, KKR, the Kuwait Investment Authority (KIA), NVIDIA, and US power company Vistra are also founding investors in Helix.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI Data Centers Evolve Into Core Platforms for a New Industrial Ecosystem</strong></h2>



<p>AI data centers are rapidly evolving beyond simple facility investments into core business platforms. Today, they integrate diverse, high-value business models, including GPU-as-a-Service (GPUaaS), Sovereign AI, and colocation services.</p>



<p>The rapid expansion of these data centers is driving the creation of a massive, multi-sector ecosystem that spans power generation, cooling technologies, semiconductors, and finance. Samsung affiliates’ investment reflects a strategic commitment to securing next-generation AI leadership within this emerging infrastructure landscape.</p>



<p>As AI services integrate deeper into daily life and global industries, the need for data centers capable of processing massive compute workloads — and the specialized power infrastructure required to run them stably — is growing exponentially. If data centers are the production hubs of the AI era, power is the fuel that drives them. Consequently, the ability to secure both compute and power infrastructure simultaneously has emerged as a defining competitive advantage in the AI industry.</p>



<p>By targeting both data center deployment and the underlying power infrastructure, Helix’s investment strategy addresses the very core of this market competition. Recognizing that power availability is currently the greatest bottleneck in AI infrastructure, Helix plans to rapidly secure energy capacity through direct investments and strategic partnerships with major energy developers, including investor Vistra.</p>



<p>The investment this time is expected to serve as a pivotal milestone for Samsung’s affiliates, allowing them to leverage their world-class hardware and component expertise to build system- and infrastructure-level influence. Moving forward, Samsung is poised to expand its role from a traditional hardware component supplier to an active architect of the global AI infrastructure ecosystem.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung’s Expertise Across the AI Infrastructure Stack Serves as Critical Anchor for Global AI Buildout</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung Electronics’ Device Solutions (DS) Division supports the global buildout with its state-of-the-art semiconductor solutions, responding to changes in global demand in a timely manner.</p>



<p>Samsung Electronics’ Device eXperience (DX) Division delivers a comprehensive portfolio of data center cooling products and solutions — from air cooling systems to coolant distribution units (CDUs) for liquid cooling — through FläktGroup, its data center HVAC specialist subsidiary acquired in 2025. It also operates 14 production sites worldwide and a supply and service network across 65 countries, giving it the speed and flexibility to meet hyperscalers’ demands for rapid capacity expansion.</p>



<p>Samsung C&T Engineering & Construction Group is actively driving the large-scale infrastructure projects as an engineering, procurement, and construction (EPC) contractor across data centers and power generation.</p>



<p>Samsung SDS is an IT solutions provider that also designs, builds and operates data centers and has recently ventured into the GPUaaS business. The company boasts the lowest power usage effectiveness (PUE) level in Korea which it applies both to its own data centers and to the Korea AI Computing Center (KOACC).</p>



<p>Samsung SDI is recognized for world-leading technology in uninterruptible power supplies (UPS) and battery backup units (BBUs), both essential to running data centers that operate high-performance servers 24 hours a day. It plans to explore new business opportunities in this field, where ultra-high-output, high-capacity battery technology is essential.</p>



<p>Through this investment, Samsung Electronics and its affiliates aim to accelerate global AI data center deployment alongside Helix, while maximizing collaborative synergies among participating companies.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[[World Heart Day] Taking Care of Your Heart Every Day, Starting From Your Wrist]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/world-heart-day-taking-care-of-your-heart-every-day-starting-from-your-wrist</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 29 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28114455/Samsung-Mobile-World-Heart-Day-Samsung-Health_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Watch]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Heart Health]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Health]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[World Heart Day]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/3VTo0u9</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[No matter who you are or what you do, we all have one constant companion we rely on every single day: our heart. Unseen but always at work, it beats around 100,000 times a day, keeping pace with us through every moment. Yet for something so essential to our lives, taking care of our hearts […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>No matter who you are or what you do, we all have one constant companion we rely on every single day: our heart. Unseen but always at work, it beats around 100,000 times a day, keeping pace with us through every moment. Yet for something so essential to our lives, taking care of our hearts is often overlooked until it’s too late.</p>



<p>Heart disease is the number one cause of death worldwide,<sup data-fn="f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81" class="fn"><a href="#f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81" id="f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81-link">1</a></sup> which is why the World Health Organization (WHO) and the World Heart Federation recognize Sept. 29 as <a href="https://world-heart-federation.org/world-heart-day/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">World Heart Day</a>. It is a reminder that the choices we make today can make a meaningful difference tomorrow.</p>



<p>These choices don’t have to be big. From choosing when to go to bed to making time for even just 30 minutes of exercise, the small choices we make every day add up to a healthier lifestyle. The good news is that Samsung Galaxy Watch can help you make proactive prevention part of daily life. By monitoring your sleep, activity level, stress, and blood pressure, Galaxy Watch helps you better understand what’s happening in your body and what steps to take to ensure your heart is doing its job well.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Understanding Your Body at a Glance: Heart Health Score</h2>



<p>The American Heart Association recommends looking at <a href="https://www.heart.org/en/healthy-living/healthy-lifestyle/lifes-essential-8" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Life’s Essential 8</a><sup data-fn="d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc" class="fn"><a href="#d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc" id="d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc-link">2</a></sup> as key measures for improving and maintaining cardiovascular health: diet, exercise, sleep, weight, blood pressure, blood sugar, cholesterol and smoking cessation. But knowing what matters raises an important question: how do these factors change the choices we make every day?</p>



<p>Samsung Health’s <strong>Heart Health Score</strong> is designed to help you in this journey. It tracks and analyzes complex health indicators such as sleep duration, daily activity level, body composition and vascular load,<sup data-fn="6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12" class="fn"><a href="#6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12" id="6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12-link">3</a></sup> converting them into a single, simple score that anyone can intuitively understand and follow.</p>



<p>Think about what happens if you miss your daily exercise for a few days: your Galaxy Watch recognizes your moderate-to-high intensity activity level has decreased, reflects this change in a lower Heart Health Score, and makes a recommendation to go for a run. With convenient insights right from your wrist, Galaxy Watch delivers personalized insights that make it easier to recognize changes, understand your habits, and take the next step toward healthier ones.</p>



<div class="wp-block-create-block-image-slider-normal"><div><div>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center"><strong>▲ Galaxy Watch and Samsung Health provide effortless understanding of cardiovascular wellness through the Heart Health Score (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)  </strong></h4>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Advanced Sensor Technology: From Measurement to Meaning</h2>



<p>Samsung Galaxy Watches are equipped with an advanced <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/how-galaxy-watchs-innovative-sensor-breaks-new-ground-in-preventative-care" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BioActive Sensor</strong></a> which integrates three precision sensors in one: an optical heart rate sensor (PPG), an electrocardiogram sensor (ECG),<sup data-fn="a2f4de84-979e-4913-990c-f2aa6b3a9ab3" class="fn"><a href="#a2f4de84-979e-4913-990c-f2aa6b3a9ab3" id="a2f4de84-979e-4913-990c-f2aa6b3a9ab3-link">4</a></sup> and a bioelectrical impedance analysis sensor (BIA).</p>



<p>With calibration just once every four weeks using a home blood pressure monitor, you can easily check your blood pressure<sup data-fn="a5904d8d-6dd2-40e7-8871-c441b3c6abad" class="fn"><a href="#a5904d8d-6dd2-40e7-8871-c441b3c6abad" id="a5904d8d-6dd2-40e7-8871-c441b3c6abad-link">5</a></sup> from your wrist anytime, anywhere. When you place your finger on the watch’s side electrode for just 30 seconds, the watch measures an electrocardiogram to detect signs of abnormal heartbeats like atrial fibrillation or ectopic beats. The<strong> Irregular Heart Rhythm Notification (IHRN)</strong><sup data-fn="145dba3e-2b35-4a53-b8ac-2b8e0c3c068e" class="fn"><a href="#145dba3e-2b35-4a53-b8ac-2b8e0c3c068e" id="145dba3e-2b35-4a53-b8ac-2b8e0c3c068e-link">6</a></sup> feature, which detects abnormal heart signals, monitors the user’s heart rhythm throughout the day and provides a proactive notification if abnormal signs are repeated.</p>



<p>Even small reminders like this have the power to save lives. In several countries, these notifications have led to <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/when-every-second-counts-galaxy-watch-series-health-features-help-save-lives" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">early detection of risks</a> such as atherosclerosis or myocardial infarction.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28113310/Samsung-Mobile-World-Heart-Day-Samsung-Health_main3.jpg" alt="With convenient insights right from your wrist, Galaxy Watch delivers personalized insights that make it easier to recognize changes, understand your habits, and take the next step toward healthier ones." class="wp-image-177479" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch supports heart rhythm monitoring through irregular heart rhythm notifications and ECG readings. (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Around-the-Clock Care: How Galaxy Watch Proactively Monitors Your Health Even During Sleep</h2>



<p>Your heart is a constant you can count on, and it works whether you’re awake or asleep. When you wear your Galaxy Watch to sleep, it analyzes five major vital signs: heart rate during sleep, heart rate variability,<sup data-fn="8352c5ec-2d95-4a28-ab78-e80450890a6d" class="fn"><a href="#8352c5ec-2d95-4a28-ab78-e80450890a6d" id="8352c5ec-2d95-4a28-ab78-e80450890a6d-link">7</a></sup> respiratory rate, skin temperature, and blood oxygen saturation. These signals are compared against your personal baseline.</p>



<p>When you wake up, your Galaxy Watch will send a notification if it detects meaningful deviations from your baseline. By highlighting meaningful changes early, it gives you an opportunity to rest, recover or seek medical guidance before small issues become larger concerns.<sup data-fn="6be7936f-f96f-454c-ae04-2ea4a2214fa0" class="fn"><a href="#6be7936f-f96f-454c-ae04-2ea4a2214fa0" id="6be7936f-f96f-454c-ae04-2ea4a2214fa0-link">8</a></sup></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28113416/Samsung-Mobile-World-Heart-Day-Samsung-Health_main4.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch and Samsung Health give users a clear view of their sleep quality with detailed metrics and insights they can use to improve their sleep habits. (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)" class="wp-image-177481" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch and Samsung Health give users a clear view of their sleep quality with detailed metrics and insights they can use to improve their sleep habits. (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Heart to Heart: Connected Care That Adds Peace of Mind</h2>



<p>Looking after your own heart is only one side of the story. From parents to partners, siblings to grandparents or even friends, the burden of caring for the hearts of those around you weighs heavily. Samsung Health’s <strong>Health Data Sharing</strong><sup data-fn="546b16ac-3748-4082-b1af-d2eb09b7f769" class="fn"><a href="#546b16ac-3748-4082-b1af-d2eb09b7f769" id="546b16ac-3748-4082-b1af-d2eb09b7f769-link">9</a></sup> function lightens the load by sharing agreed-upon data items (number of steps, blood pressure, heart rate, medication adherence, etc.) with loved ones.</p>



<p>You can check if your parents who live far away have completed their daily walk or taken their prescribed medication on time, just as if you were right there with them.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28113501/Samsung-Mobile-World-Heart-Day-Samsung-Health_main5.jpg" alt="Samsung Health lets users share selected health data with loved ones, helping them stay connected and informed about one another’s wellbeing. (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)" class="wp-image-177483" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Health lets users share selected health data with loved ones, helping them stay connected and informed about one another’s wellbeing. (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)</figcaption></figure>



<p>World Heart Day coincides with the seasonal transition when chilly winds begin to blow in the mornings and evenings. As the temperature gap between day and night widens, blood vessels constrict, sharply increasing the risk of cardiovascular disease.</p>



<p>The key to preventing heart disease lies not in reactive treatment, but in proactive habits of paying close attention to the subtle signals your body sends every day. Start managing your heart health with Galaxy Watch, a reliable health partner that stays with you around the clock.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

It continuously analyzes changes during sleep to provide a comprehensive score. <a href="#6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 3"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="a2f4de84-979e-4913-990c-f2aa6b3a9ab3">Electrocardiogram (ECG) feature records and analyzes subtle electrical signals of the heart when the user places a finger on Galaxy Watch’s top button for about 30 seconds, detecting atrial fibrillation (irregular and rapid quivering) and ectopic beats (premature irregular beats). <a href="#a2f4de84-979e-4913-990c-f2aa6b3a9ab3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 4"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="a5904d8d-6dd2-40e7-8871-c441b3c6abad">Blood Pressure feature measures systolic and diastolic blood pressure by analyzing pulse waveforms with Galaxy Watch’s optical (PPG) sensor. An initial measurement using a separate cuff-type blood pressure monitor is required, and recalibration is needed every 4 weeks (28 days). <a href="#a5904d8d-6dd2-40e7-8871-c441b3c6abad-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 5"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="145dba3e-2b35-4a53-b8ac-2b8e0c3c068e">Irregular Heart Rhythm Notification (IHRN) feature detects irregular patterns that may indicate atrial fibrillation through pulse monitoring and provides an early alert. <a href="#145dba3e-2b35-4a53-b8ac-2b8e0c3c068e-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 6"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="8352c5ec-2d95-4a28-ab78-e80450890a6d">Heart Rate Variability (HRV) feature analyzes minute-to-minute variations in the intervals between heartbeats to show autonomic nervous system balance. It is automatically recorded during sleep and resting states. <a href="#8352c5ec-2d95-4a28-ab78-e80450890a6d-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 7"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="6be7936f-f96f-454c-ae04-2ea4a2214fa0">Heart Health Score and Vitals features are supported on the Samsung Health app (version 7.0 or higher). <a href="#6be7936f-f96f-454c-ae04-2ea4a2214fa0-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 8"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="546b16ac-3748-4082-b1af-d2eb09b7f769">Health Data Sharing feature requires user consent, and users can independently choose which health data to share (including steps, workouts, sleep, weight, body composition, food intake, heart rate, stress, blood pressure, blood glucose, medication and vital signs). <a href="#546b16ac-3748-4082-b1af-d2eb09b7f769-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 9"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

<p>Samsung Art Store<sup data-fn="3cb85577-cb41-4568-bfc6-52abd6835faa" class="fn"><a href="#3cb85577-cb41-4568-bfc6-52abd6835faa" id="3cb85577-cb41-4568-bfc6-52abd6835faa-link">1</a></sup> has announced its first-ever cinematic collection, featuring 38 stills from acclaimed films produced by studio A24. Available on supported Samsung Art TVs worldwide, the collection allows subscribers to revisit memorable moments and discover new favorites at home.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/23102547/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Cinematic-Collection-A24_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Art Store has announced its first-ever cinematic collection, featuring 38 stills from acclaimed films produced by studio A24. <p>A24 is a global entertainment company that produces and distributes films, TV series, documentaries, music, and consumer products. Notable films include “Everything Everywhere All at Once,” “Moonlight,” “Past Lives,” and “Midsommar.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/23102617/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Cinematic-Collection-A24_main2.jpg" alt="Notable films include “Everything Everywhere All at Once,” “Moonlight,” “Past Lives,” and “Midsommar.”" class="wp-image-177412" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ A still from “I Saw the TV Glow,” courtesy of A24</figcaption></figure>



<p>“Our collaboration with A24 expands the horizons of Samsung Art Store by bringing powerful cinematic storytelling to TV screens,” said Heeyeong Ahn, Vice President of the Visual Display (VD) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We look forward to seeing these iconic film moments inspire audiences around the world and offer a new way to engage with the stories they love.”</p>



<p>Designed to serve as modern digital canvases, <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/tvs/art-tv/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Art TVs</a> display artwork with vibrant color and rich detail. The Frame, along with The Frame Pro — whose debut model last year was named one of <a href="https://time.com/collections/best-inventions-2025/7318290/samsung-frame-pro/?filters=Design" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">TIME’s “Best Inventions”</a> — blend seamlessly into home décor like picture frames. The Frame lineup comes in a range of sizes, with customizable bezel options to help transform any space into a gallery.</p>



<p>Samsung further expanded its Art TV lineup<sup data-fn="7eed3a34-b207-4aa3-8fca-b4657b0c2758" class="fn"><a href="#7eed3a34-b207-4aa3-8fca-b4657b0c2758" id="7eed3a34-b207-4aa3-8fca-b4657b0c2758-link">2</a></sup> in 2026, offering Samsung Art Store to its Micro RGB and Neo QLED series. Samsung Art Store is now available on select Samsung OLED TVs<sup data-fn="b0794d78-aff5-4a44-b3af-1b2c12079bdc" class="fn"><a href="#b0794d78-aff5-4a44-b3af-1b2c12079bdc" id="b0794d78-aff5-4a44-b3af-1b2c12079bdc-link">3</a></sup> for the first time, including the flagship S95H.</p>



<p>Samsung Art Store is the No. 1 TV art subscription service globally, reaching over 117 countries. Subscribers can access more than 5,000 artworks from over 800 artists, including pieces from collaborations with institutions such as The Metropolitan Museum of Art, MoMA, and Musée d’Orsay.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung, SK Telecom, and Hana Financial Group Build Korea’s First Private 5G Smart Office in the Financial Sector]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-sk-telecom-and-hana-financial-group-buildkoreas-first-private-5g-smart-office-in-the-financial-sector</link>
				<pubDate>Mon, 28 Sep 2026 08:30:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/23152100/Samsung-Network-Solutions-Private-5G-Smart-Office-SK-Telecom-Hana-Financial-Group_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Network Solutions]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Hana Financial Group]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[SK Telecom]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Smart Office]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4h9EyX9</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that the company serves as the sole vendor to deliver its private 5G network solutions, powering a smart office at Hana Financial Group’s new headquarters in Incheon, South Korea. As the first of its kind in the Korean financial sector, this new connected office elevates convenience in mobility and provides a […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that the company serves as the sole vendor to deliver its private 5G network solutions, powering a smart office at Hana Financial Group’s new headquarters in Incheon, South Korea. As the first of its kind in the Korean financial sector, this new connected office elevates convenience in mobility and provides a foundation for enterprise AI transformation (AX) while meeting the stringent security requirements of this industry.</p>



<p>In collaboration with SK Telecom and Samsung, Hana Financial Group built out a large-scale smart office, spanning 16 floors of its headquarters, multiple group affiliates, and thousands of employees. Using 5G-enabled laptops, tablets, and mobile devices, employees can access a seamless work environment anytime, anywhere. This enhanced connectivity drives more efficient use of office space, supports employee productivity, and fosters a more agile way of working, while reducing the time and resources needed to reconfigure the building. It also lays the groundwork for adopting AI across daily operations.</p>



<p>Financial institutions handle highly sensitive data such as customers’ credit and bank information, and operate under strict network-separation requirements — leaving Wi-Fi adoption largely off-limits over security concerns and cyber risks. This project deploys a multi-layered security system, encompassing enhanced encryption, a firewall, DDoS protection, and USIM-based authentication — granting network access exclusively to authorized devices and registered users. Samsung’s private 5G solutions offer both the complete isolation of wired networks and the convenience of wireless connectivity.</p>



<p>“For AI and data to be used widely in day-to-day work, employees need a network that gives them secure, reliable access to internal systems and data from anywhere,” said Kim Goo-Young, Head of Enterprise Business Office at SK Telecom. “Built on Samsung’s core and radio solutions, that secure foundation is now in place — making this smart office a leading example of how we innovate the way the financial sector works.”</p>



<p>For this project, Samsung provided its full stack of network solutions including radios, baseband, and cloud-native core, supporting the mid-band (4.7GHz) spectrum dedicated to private 5G networks in Korea. Samsung’s 5G Standalone Core enables network slicing, which divides a single physical network infrastructure into multiple virtual slices where each is dedicated to a specific application. Through this deployment, Hana Financial Group’s subsidiaries will each have access to separate network slices, offering independent operating environments tailored to individual security policies and business needs.</p>



<p>“This meaningful milestone underscores Samsung’s leadership in deploying private 5G solutions that deliver even under the most stringent security standards required by regulated industries,” said Angelo Jeongho Park, Executive Vice President and Head of Strategic Sales, Networks Business at Samsung Electronics. “By combining cutting-edge technology and our extensive commercial experiences across diverse industries — including manufacturing, finance, construction, IT, and more — we are providing secure, tailored networks today while enabling businesses to seamlessly integrate AI-driven innovations.”</p>



<p>Samsung Networks has pioneered the successful delivery of 5G end-to-end solutions, including chipsets, radios, and cores. Through ongoing research and development, Samsung is driving the industry to advance 5G networks and paving the way for 6G and beyond with its market-leading product portfolio, from purpose-built RAN, vRAN, Open RAN, AI RAN, and cloud-native AI core to private network solutions and AI-powered automation tools (CognitiV NOS). The company currently provides innovative network solutions to mobile operators that deliver boundless connectivity to hundreds of millions of users worldwide.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung at the Forefront of Korea’s AI RAN Projects with KT and SK Telecom]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-at-the-forefront-of-koreas-ai-ran-projects-with-kt-and-sk-telecom</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 23 Sep 2026 22:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22145335/Samsung-Network-Solutions-AI-RAN-Projects-KT-SK-Telecom_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Network Solutions]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[6G]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[AI-RAN]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[KT]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[SK Telecom]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4xucwKN</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced it has signed contracts with KT and SK Telecom for their AI RAN projects under the Korean Ministry of Science and ICT’s (MSIT) “Hyper AI Network” initiative, taking on the roles of sole global vendor for KT and main vendor for SK Telecom. Building on its decades-long partnerships with the country’s […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced it has signed contracts with KT and SK Telecom for their AI RAN projects under the Korean Ministry of Science and ICT’s (MSIT) “Hyper AI Network” initiative, taking on the roles of sole global vendor for KT and main vendor for SK Telecom. Building on its decades-long partnerships with the country’s leading operators and its leadership in software-driven, AI-native network innovation, Samsung is the only partner selected for both projects.</p>



<p>Set to begin in October 2026, the projects will deploy 5G Standalone (SA) private networks across industrial environments, ranging from shipyards to petrochemical facilities, to validate AI RAN capabilities and generate real-world insights for future deployments.</p>



<p>As part of the Korean MSIT’s initiative, the projects bring together operators, network solution providers, and other industry partners. The collaboration aims to accelerate the adoption of cutting-edge technologies, including AI RAN, physical AI, and autonomous operations, while laying the groundwork for the transition to 6G.</p>



<p>“Korea has long stood at the forefront of global digital and technological advancement. As a key partner with deep roots in this region, we are proud to drive the next stage of network innovation alongside leading operators through these AI RAN projects,” said June Moon, Executive Vice President, Head of R&D, Networks Business at Samsung Electronics. “As AI evolves toward embodied intelligence, networks are becoming platforms that connect a new generation of intelligent machines, including humanoids and autonomous vehicles. Bringing AI RAN and physical AI together in real-world industrial settings marks a meaningful step toward the future of connectivity.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI-Powered Welding Robots at Shipyards</strong></h2>



<p>At the Yeongam Shipyard of HD Hyundai Samho, a prominent global shipbuilder, KT will carry out field trials of key physical AI services, ranging from welding and painting robots in manufacturing environments to autonomous robots for telecom facility operations.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22164247/Samsung-Network-Solutions-AI-RAN-Projects-KT-SK-Telecom_main1.jpg" alt="For the AI-powered quadruped welding robot use case, Samsung will support KT with its software-driven, AI-native solutions, including Network in a Server (NIS)." class="wp-image-177400" /></figure>



<p>For the AI-powered quadruped welding robot use case, Samsung will support KT with its software-driven, AI-native solutions, including Network in a Server (NIS) — an integrated edge AI platform comprising virtualized RAN (vRAN), AI Core, and AI applications — and Samsung CognitiV Network Operations Suite (NOS) for intelligent network automation. Leveraging 5G connectivity and the edge AI platform, the quadruped robot can autonomously perceive its working environment and perform precise welding.</p>



<p>With these capabilities, Samsung is addressing the demanding network requirements of physical AI services in industrial settings, where ultra-low latency, high reliability, and consistent throughput are critical for real-time robotic control. Designed for on-premise deployment and local processing, NIS brings network and AI capabilities closer to where data is generated and decisions are made. It runs vRAN and 5G SA core software on an AMD EPYC<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> 8004 Server CPU-powered commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) Compact Edge Server from Supermicro with Wind River Cloud Platform. Its software-driven architecture also enables flexible scaling, with the ability to incorporate accelerated GPU compute as needed to meet site-specific requirements. </p>



<p>“KT views this project as an important starting point for validating the next-generation communications paradigm where AI and networks converge,” said Jongsik Lee, Executive Vice President, KT. “Together with Samsung, we will demonstrate a range of Physical AI services in an AI-RAN-enabled private network environment and secure the key technologies and service models that will lead the AI-Native 6G era.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Autonomous Patrol Robots at Petrochemical Facilities</strong></h2>



<p>As another key part of the country’s Hyper AI Network initiative, SK Telecom will validate physical AI services at SK Incheon Petrochem, a major energy hub for the Seoul metropolitan area. These capabilities include an autonomous robot that patrols hazardous areas and CCTV-based monitoring. The patrol robot transfers high-definition videos in real time while on the move, enabling AI analysis to detect potential hazards and facilitate integrated monitoring.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22164312/Samsung-Network-Solutions-AI-RAN-Projects-KT-SK-Telecom_main2.jpg" alt="Samsung is providing its AI-native NIS solution for SK Telecom’s project. " class="wp-image-177401" /></figure>



<p>Samsung is providing its AI-native NIS solution for SK Telecom’s project. The companies will explore how AI-native connectivity can further enhance safety and efficiency in petrochemical environments, where complex chemical processes require rigorous monitoring and operational precision.</p>



<p>“At SK Telecom, we believe AI creates greater value when paired with the right foundation to support it, and this project puts that belief into practice,” said Takki Yu, Head of Network Technology Office at SK Telecom.<strong> </strong>“Drawing on our AI expertise, we will work with Samsung to validate physical AI services in real-world environments and advance AI RAN as a key technology underpinning the government’s Hyper AI Network initiative and its AI Highway agenda.”</p>



<p>Samsung’s AI RAN delivers a range of capabilities, including anomaly detection, throughput enhancement, and energy efficiency improvements, all validated on live networks around the globe. Building on this experience, Samsung continues to work with leading operators and partners to expand the real-world footprint of AI-native, 6G-ready networks and propel the adoption of AI-driven services across global markets.</p>



<p>Samsung Networks has pioneered the successful delivery of 5G end-to-end solutions, including chipsets, radios, and cores. Through ongoing research and development, Samsung is driving the industry to advance 5G networks and paving the way for 6G and beyond with its market-leading product portfolio, from purpose-built RAN, vRAN, Open RAN, AI RAN, and cloud-native AI core to private network solutions and AI-powered automation tools (CognitiV NOS). The company currently provides innovative network solutions to mobile operators that deliver boundless connectivity to hundreds of millions of users worldwide.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/bringing-fantasy-characters-to-life-samsung-spatial-signage-transforms-tokyo-game-show-2026</link>

								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/23075626/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />

									<description><![CDATA[At Tokyo Game Show (TGS) 2026, held from September 17 to 20 at Makuhari Messe in Chiba, Japan, Samsung Electronics and NC demonstrated the future of fan engagement by bringing the characters of Astrae Oratio — the upcoming urban fantasy RPG — into the physical world through glasses-free 3D Spatial Signage. The installation transformed a […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>At Tokyo Game Show (TGS) 2026, held from September 17 to 20 at Makuhari Messe in Chiba, Japan, Samsung Electronics and NC demonstrated the future of fan engagement by bringing the characters of <a href="https://astraeoratio.plaync.com/en-us/world" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><em>Astrae Oratio</em></a> — the upcoming urban fantasy RPG — into the physical world through glasses-free 3D Spatial Signage.</p>



<p>The installation transformed a conventional booth into an immersive experience where visitors encountered lifelike characters at scale, blurring the line between digital worlds and physical spaces.</p>



<div class="youtube_wrap"><iframe loading="lazy" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/XuRaGIAJTZc" width="300" height="150" title="YouTube video" allowfullscreen></iframe></div>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Extending Fan Engagement Into Physical Spaces</strong></h2>



<p>For game publishers and IP owners, the challenge lies in translating digital characters into physical environments.</p>



<p>At the entrance to the booth of NC, a global game developer and publishing company, an 85-inch Spatial Signage display was integrated into an elevator-themed installation and introduced <em>Astrae Oratio</em> characters at near life-size scale. Featuring Samsung’s patented 3D Plate technology, Spatial Signage created a vivid sense of depth without requiring viewers to wear 3D glasses, bringing the game’s characters and its magical world into the physical space.</p>



<p>“We wanted visitors to come face-to-face with the characters at life-size scale and feel immersed in the game’s world from the moment they entered the booth,” said Yongsoo Lee, Head of Astrae Oratio Business Division at NC. “For games in the subculture genre, the liveliness and realism of the characters are especially important. We believed that Samsung Spatial Signage would make this possible.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22153942/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main1.jpg" alt="The 85-inch Spatial Signage created the impression that characters from Astrae Oratio are stepping out of an elevator into the real world." class="wp-image-177375" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22154000/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main2.jpg" alt="The 85-inch Spatial Signage created the impression that characters from Astrae Oratio are stepping out of an elevator into the real world." class="wp-image-177376" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption"> ▲ The 85-inch Spatial Signage created the impression that characters from <em>Astrae Oratio</em> are stepping out of an elevator into the real world.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Visitors responded immediately to the display’s scale, movement, and sense of depth.</p>



<p>Content creator Kawaii CoCo noted, “The character felt interactive because she was moving around, which made her feel a little more human-like. That really caught my attention.” She added that the display’s social media presence was undeniable. “It’s much more prominent and looks much better on social media, so I wanted to capture some footage and show my audience this really cool display.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22154108/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main3.jpg" alt="Content creator Kawaii CoCo emphasized how the character’s lifelike movement made the display feel interactive and eye-catching for social media. " class="wp-image-177377" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Content creator Kawaii CoCo emphasized how the character’s lifelike movement made the display feel interactive and eye-catching for social media.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Despite its slim 52mm profile,<sup data-fn="a731a826-7470-4400-adf9-b71c859104cc" class="fn"><a href="#a731a826-7470-4400-adf9-b71c859104cc" id="a731a826-7470-4400-adf9-b71c859104cc-link">1</a></sup> Spatial Signage is designed to create the visual impression of depth, making it appear as though the space extends behind the screen. This immersive effect creates a powerful impression when viewers first encounter a game and its characters in public settings.</p>



<p>Booth visitor Daniel Acero said, “Having this as the first impression of the game in public is really helpful for shaping public perception of the game. It also conveys the personalities of the characters.” He added, “I didn’t realize it was a display screen. I thought it was a transparent screen with an actual space behind it. It really created a strong sense of depth.”</p>



<p>Beyond the entrance, compact 32-inch Spatial Signage displays, with an even slimmer profile of 49.4mm, were placed in waiting areas and along the visitor route, extending the character experience throughout the booth. The smaller form factor enabled flexible installation in space-constrained areas while helping visitors remain engaged with the game’s characters and its world as they waited.</p>



<p>Visitor Tomas Portier said, “Even if the queue was kind of long, Spatial Signage helped the time pass more quickly while helping me get to know the characters a little better. It’s more lively than a still image or a conventional screen.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22154942/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main4.gif" alt="A compact 32-inch Spatial Signage display presented an Astrae Oratio character in the booth’s waiting area, extending the experience along the visitor route." class="wp-image-177380" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ A compact 32-inch Spatial Signage display presented an<em> Astrae Oratio</em> character in the booth’s waiting area, extending the experience along the visitor route.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Creating Visual Impact While Maximizing Space</strong></h2>



<p>For retailers and venue operators, visual impact needs to work within the realities of physical space. Spatial Signage’s slim profile, combined with glasses-free 3D presentation, enabled NC to make strategic use of limited space.</p>



<p>“Designing the visitor flow was quite challenging, but since Spatial Signage is very slim, it was really easy to install and make effective use of the space,” Yongsoo Lee explained. “That also gave us room to store merchandise for visitors.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22155024/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main5.jpg" alt="Yongsoo Lee, Head of Astrae Oratio Business Division at NC." class="wp-image-177381" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Yongsoo Lee, Head of Astrae Oratio Business Division at NC.</figcaption></figure>



<p>At the booth entrance, NC used the installation as both a visual focal point and a functional part of the layout. While the display captured visitors’ attention, the area behind it provided concealed storage for merchandise.</p>



<p>Visitor David Yousefi observed, “When you present it like this in a box, I thought it was much larger.” He added, “But it’s pretty cool that you can put it all into such a limited space.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22155140/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main6.jpg" alt="(From left) David Yousefi and Nicolas Felix experienced the striking depth of an Astrae Oratio character, delivered through Spatial Signage’s slim, space-saving design. " class="wp-image-177382" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) David Yousefi and Nicolas Felix experienced the striking depth of an <em>Astrae Oratio</em> character, delivered through Spatial Signage’s slim, space-saving design.</figcaption></figure>



<p>This flexibility could help character IP owners, retailers, and venue operators bring content into a wider range of physical environments.</p>



<p>Miguel Moren, a gaming industry professional and TGS 2026 exhibitor, said, “Having animated video game characters in a real-life space creates a really nice blend of the imaginary world and the real world.”</p>



<p>Visitors expressed enthusiasm about potential applications beyond gaming events.</p>



<p>Rio Matsushita suggested, “I think having your favorite character move right in front of you is a really great experience. Even while waiting, I could clearly see the three-dimensional visuals. I think it would be a lot of fun if we could see something like that in places like Akihabara too.”</p>



<p>Nicolas Felix also saw other opportunities for Spatial Signage. “I think it could be used for commercial branding in shops, for example, to showcase clothes on people,” he said. Yousefi also pointed to additional applications in other public-facing environments. “I could also see it being successful in theme parks or other events where you want to showcase something.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22155223/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main7.jpg" alt="Visitor Rio Matsushita took photos of the characters appearing on Spatial Signage while waiting in line to play the game." class="wp-image-177383" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/22155241/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Spatial-Signage-Tokyo-Game-Show-2026_Main8.jpg" alt="Visitor Rio Matsushita took photos of the characters appearing on Spatial Signage while waiting in line to play the game." class="wp-image-177384" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitor Rio Matsushita took photos of the characters appearing on Spatial Signage while waiting in line to play the game.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Beyond Tokyo Game Show: Expanding the Possibilities of Spatial Signage</strong></h2>



<p>The Tokyo Game Show installation is one of several commercial deployments of Samsung Spatial Signage across entertainment, retail, and event environments. At <a href="https://bit.ly/4tkZYUc" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Everland Safari World in Korea</a>, it was integrated into the attraction’s waiting queue, extending the safari experience into areas traditionally reserved for crowd management. The <a href="https://news.samsung.com/us/samsung-immersive-display-solutions-governors-ball-music-festival-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Governors Ball Music Festival</a> in the United States showcased merchandise on an 85-inch display, integrating commerce into the live-event experience.</p>



<p>These installations demonstrate how Spatial Signage brings digital content to physical space through a combination of visual impact, spatial efficiency, and visitor engagement. With a range of display sizes and a form factor designed to integrate seamlessly into existing environments, the technology enables storytelling opportunities in spaces where conventional displays have been limited.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="a731a826-7470-4400-adf9-b71c859104cc">85-inch <a href="#a731a826-7470-4400-adf9-b71c859104cc-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Opens Built-In Appliance Showroom in Germany To Advance European Market Expansion]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-opens-built-in-appliance-showroom-in-germany-to-advance-european-market-expansion</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 22 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/21111410/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Kuchenmeile-A30-Extractor-Induction-Hob-Bespoke-AI-Dishwater-Showroom-in-Germany_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced the opening of a new built-in appliance showroom in Löhne, Germany. Located on the ground floor of the Kitchen Center Löhne — an exhibition center for kitchen industry leaders — the permanent showroom provides kitchen retailers, planners, buyers, and other industry partners with year-round access to Samsung’s built-in appliance portfolio and […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/21111536/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Kuchenmeile-A30-Extractor-Induction-Hob-Bespoke-AI-Dishwater-Showroom-in-Germany_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung's built-in appliance showroom in Löhne, Germany" class="wp-image-177360" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced the opening of a new built-in appliance showroom in Löhne, Germany.</p>



<p>Located on the ground floor of the Kitchen Center Löhne — an exhibition center for kitchen industry leaders — the permanent showroom provides kitchen retailers, planners, buyers, and other industry partners with year-round access to Samsung’s built-in appliance portfolio and connected kitchen solutions.</p>



<p>“The opening of this permanent showroom marks an important step for our commitment to the built-in appliance business in Europe,” said Sang Jik Lee, Executive Vice President and Head of Sales & Marketing Team of Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We aim to deepen industry collaboration, strengthen Samsung’s premium positioning, and better capture opportunities in the European built-in market.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bringing the AI Connected Kitchen to Life</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/21111607/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Kuchenmeile-A30-Extractor-Induction-Hob-Bespoke-AI-Dishwater-Showroom-in-Germany_main2.jpg" alt="Key products on display include the Extractor Induction Hob, the Bespoke AI Dishwasher, refrigerators and the Bespoke AI Oven." class="wp-image-177361" /></figure>



<p>Designed around Samsung’s connected appliances, the showroom moves beyond conventional product displays by placing built-in appliances in realistic kitchen and residential environments. Trade visitors can experience how Samsung appliances, AI-powered features, and SmartThings connectivity work together to create more intuitive and connected experiences throughout the home. They can also see how seamlessly Samsung’s built-in appliances fit into kitchens in different price segments, from entry to premium price levels.</p>



<p>The showroom includes various areas designed to present Samsung’s built-in appliances.</p>



<p>Near the entrance, kitchen and residential environments demonstrate how Samsung appliances and SmartThings-connected devices can work together throughout the home in realistic settings. In particular, three distinct built-in kitchen packages allow visitors to easily identify and compare differences across Samsung’s product lineup. The displays also highlight how the company’s built-in kitchen appliances complement a variety of kitchen furniture concepts, including wood, white, and navy finishes.</p>



<p>Next, a dedicated product zone presents the company’s refrigeration, cooking, and dishwashing portfolios alongside lifestyle displays and select new products. Key products on display include <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-expands-kitchen-portfolio-with-intelligent-performance-and-refined-design" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">the Extractor Induction Hob</a> — Samsung’s induction cooktop with an integrated extractor which launched this year — <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-expands-kitchen-portfolio-with-intelligent-performance-and-refined-design" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">the Bespoke AI Dishwasher featuring AI Wash</a>,<sup data-fn="f4b9bb4a-9296-49b4-bba1-f5312a11e811" class="fn"><a href="#f4b9bb4a-9296-49b4-bba1-f5312a11e811" id="f4b9bb4a-9296-49b4-bba1-f5312a11e811-link">1</a></sup> Samsung’s built-in and freestanding refrigerators, and the Bespoke AI Oven featuring AI Pro Cooking.<sup data-fn="d0ad5341-e8da-47fe-a475-bdf53d612710" class="fn"><a href="#d0ad5341-e8da-47fe-a475-bdf53d612710" id="d0ad5341-e8da-47fe-a475-bdf53d612710-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>At the center of the showroom, an open consultation and communication space supports product demonstrations, partner meetings, briefings, and training programs. Visitors can also receive an introduction to Samsung’s B2B platform SPACE, including information on ordering processes, product availability, and product specifications. Together, the areas allow industry professionals to explore Samsung’s built-in portfolio while experiencing the value of its connected-home vision firsthand.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Strategic Hub for Samsung’s European Built-In Appliance Business</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung’s new showroom is also serving as the company’s exhibition space for its participation in <a href="https://www.km-kuechenmeile.de/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Küchenmeile A30</a>, a kitchen trade fair that is one of Europe’s leading B2B platforms for the kitchen, furniture, and interior design industries.</p>



<p>Held every September in and around Löhne — a region in which approximately two-thirds of Germany’s kitchen industry is concentrated — the event brings together more than 300 major kitchen furniture and appliance brands from around the world. It attracts more than 25,000 visitors annually.</p>



<p>Samsung has participated in Küchenmeile A30 since 2013, consistently using the platform to introduce its latest innovation in built-in products. This year, the company’s new showroom provides a novel experience for the wide range of stakeholders visiting the event.</p>



<p>Initially focused on Germany, the showroom is positioned to become a regional demonstration, training, and collaboration hub for Samsung teams and kitchen-industry partners across Europe.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="f4b9bb4a-9296-49b4-bba1-f5312a11e811">AI Wash is a cycle that utilizes a high-resolution turbidity sensor and a machine learning algorithm to optimize washing and rinsing. It operates based on an AI-created algorithm. Actual results may vary depending on individual use. <a href="#f4b9bb4a-9296-49b4-bba1-f5312a11e811-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="d0ad5341-e8da-47fe-a475-bdf53d612710">AI Pro Cooking is a feature that uses an internal camera and AI to support cooking functions such as monitoring. Only available on certain models. <a href="#d0ad5341-e8da-47fe-a475-bdf53d612710-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Begins Official Rollout of One UI 9, Bringing the Latest Galaxy Experiences to More Devices]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-begins-official-rollout-of-one-ui-9-bringing-the-latest-galaxy-experiences-to-more-devices</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 16 Sep 2026 21:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16160212/Samsung-Mobile-Official-Rollout-One-UI-9_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[My FanCam]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Now Brief]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Now Nudge]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[One UI]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[One UI 9]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4h7IpD0</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced the expansion of One UI 9 to more Galaxy devices, with the rollout beginning today starting with the Galaxy S26, S26+, and S26 Ultra. Following its debut on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8, and Flip8, One UI 9 brings an easier, more seamless mobile experience across form factors, as well as […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced the expansion of One UI 9 to more Galaxy devices, with the rollout beginning today starting with the Galaxy S26, S26+, and S26 Ultra.<sup data-fn="fc4674b2-7437-41ad-a3ed-0b13071815da" class="fn"><a href="#fc4674b2-7437-41ad-a3ed-0b13071815da" id="fc4674b2-7437-41ad-a3ed-0b13071815da-link">1</a></sup> Following its <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-z-fold8-ultra-fold8-and-flip8foldables-perfected-for-every-way-of-living" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">debut</a> on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8, and Flip8, One UI 9 brings an easier, more seamless mobile experience across form factors, as well as enhanced Galaxy AI that is richer, more personal, and more helpful.</p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>New Features Help Users Capture, Personalize, and Manage</strong></h2>



<p>One UI 9 introduces new ways to keep people in focus, tailor the information users see each day, and manage device care more easily.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16151836/Samsung-Mobile-Official-Rollout-One-UI-9_Main1.mp4"></video></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>My FanCam</strong><sup data-fn="e114fb93-6eb4-4efc-a487-fabd4aae1aae" class="fn"><a href="#e114fb93-6eb4-4efc-a487-fabd4aae1aae" id="e114fb93-6eb4-4efc-a487-fabd4aae1aae-link">2</a></sup><strong> </strong>automatically tracks a selected person in a recorded video and keeps them centered, intelligently reframing the footage as the scene moves. This allows users to turn dynamic moments into social-ready content with less manual editing. Whether it’s a child’s performance, a friend on the dance floor, or an exciting moment during a sporting event, users can instantly put the focus on the person they want to see.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16152218/Samsung-Mobile-Official-Rollout-One-UI-9_Main2.mp4"></video></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The new <strong>Generative and Editable Cards</strong> on <strong>Now Brief</strong><sup data-fn="1af1cb8e-1c93-47c5-8e19-043cf24d37cd" class="fn"><a href="#1af1cb8e-1c93-47c5-8e19-043cf24d37cd" id="1af1cb8e-1c93-47c5-8e19-043cf24d37cd-link">3</a></sup> help users choose what appears in their daily summaries. Users can create cards across health, weather, and video so their daily brief reflects the information most relevant to them.</li>



<li><strong>Warranty and Care</strong><sup data-fn="e150a367-0294-4d32-b2b6-83b5d0689a84" class="fn"><a href="#e150a367-0294-4d32-b2b6-83b5d0689a84" id="e150a367-0294-4d32-b2b6-83b5d0689a84-link">4</a></sup> brings device support and protection directly into the Settings menu. Users can now check warranty coverage across connected mobile devices, run self-diagnostics, view repair estimates, and manage Samsung Care+ or book a service appointment with just a few taps.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Enhanced Capabilities Help Users Do More With Less Effort</strong></h2>



<p>One UI 9 builds on familiar Galaxy experiences to help users complete everyday tasks in fewer steps, with context-aware suggestions, timely creative prompts, and improvements to translation, scanning, and recording.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16153313/Samsung-Mobile-Official-Rollout-One-UI-9_Main3.jpg" alt="The feature of Now Nudge " class="wp-image-177340" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Now Nudge</strong><sup data-fn="db8838b9-cdbb-4ac9-a5cf-19578ee0a35e" class="fn"><a href="#db8838b9-cdbb-4ac9-a5cf-19578ee0a35e" id="db8838b9-cdbb-4ac9-a5cf-19578ee0a35e-link">5</a></sup> can surface timely, context-aware suggestions across more moments in the Galaxy experience, helping users take the next step without breaking their flow. For example, when making plans over text, users can pull up reservation details or save a shared location without leaving the conversation.</li>



<li><strong>Creative Studio</strong> now suggests image-generation ideas based on relevant upcoming events. For example, when a friend’s birthday is approaching, users may see a prompt to create a themed image or greeting, allowing them to quickly create something meaningful for the occasion.</li>



<li><strong>Interpreter</strong><sup data-fn="baa85802-65cf-4171-89dd-944d26fdcece" class="fn"><a href="#baa85802-65cf-4171-89dd-944d26fdcece" id="baa85802-65cf-4171-89dd-944d26fdcece-link">6</a></sup> introduces a new Thread View that enables users to better follow translated conversations by quickly referencing earlier exchanges. In addition, faster translation in Conversation Mode and quicker playback with compatible Galaxy Buds help reduce delays, making conversations feel more natural and continuous.</li>



<li><strong>Document Scan</strong> now allows users to digitize challenging materials such as curved book pages and corner-folded papers with less manual adjustment. The large-screen-optimized views on compatible Galaxy devices, along with an improved Finger Eraser, help create cleaner and more usable scans.</li>



<li><strong>Voice Recorder</strong> presents summaries in a clearer, more structured format, using elements such as headings and tables to make key information easier to scan at a glance.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Enhanced Security and Privacy Help Users Stay Informed and in Control</strong></h2>



<p>One UI 9 continues Samsung’s approach to security and privacy with transparency, choice, and control built into the experience. The latest updates help users better understand when something may need their attention and make it easier to take action.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Security Brief</strong> brings key security and privacy updates like alerts for malware, apps from unknown sources, and unnecessary permissions to Now Brief, helping users identify potential issues earlier and determine when action is necessary.</li>



<li><strong>Privacy Alerts</strong><sup data-fn="895757f6-088a-42ce-98e8-027366e38545" class="fn"><a href="#895757f6-088a-42ce-98e8-027366e38545" id="895757f6-088a-42ce-98e8-027366e38545-link">7</a></sup> use on-device AI to identify potential privacy risks, including unnecessary attempts to access permissions in the background, giving users greater awareness of how their information is being accessed.</li>



<li><strong>Scam Detection</strong><sup data-fn="d1323b70-edab-48bf-9a07-6bc6b9a1cd02" class="fn"><a href="#d1323b70-edab-48bf-9a07-6bc6b9a1cd02" id="d1323b70-edab-48bf-9a07-6bc6b9a1cd02-link">8</a></sup> expands to additional countries<sup data-fn="a1a83f94-0edb-4a3b-aede-32e57a2b7090" class="fn"><a href="#a1a83f94-0edb-4a3b-aede-32e57a2b7090" id="a1a83f94-0edb-4a3b-aede-32e57a2b7090-link">9</a></sup> and languages, bringing protection against potential scam calls to more Galaxy users across supported markets.</li>
</ul>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="fc4674b2-7437-41ad-a3ed-0b13071815da">As of the publication of this press release, the official One UI 9 has been rolled out to Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8. The update is expected to gradually roll out to other Galaxy devices. <a href="#fc4674b2-7437-41ad-a3ed-0b13071815da-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="e114fb93-6eb4-4efc-a487-fabd4aae1aae">My FanCam feature requires a Samsung Account login. Availability may vary by video. Not supported if there is no subject to track. Accuracy of results is not guaranteed. <a href="#e114fb93-6eb4-4efc-a487-fabd4aae1aae-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="1af1cb8e-1c93-47c5-8e19-043cf24d37cd">Now Brief feature requires Samsung Account login.
