					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[[Interview] The People Behind the Galaxy Z Series’ Camera Innovations ②: How Portrait Video Recreates the Look of Professional Camera Lenses]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/interview-the-people-behind-the-galaxy-z-series-camera-innovations-%e2%91%a1-how-portrait-video-recreates-the-look-of-professional-camera-lenses</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 16 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15174444/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Technology]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Flip8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Interview]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Portrait Video]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4AeT9rQ</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Unveiled at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, Samsung Electronics’ Galaxy Z series introduces My FanCam — a new feature that lets users focus their edits on a person of their choice — as well as an enhanced Portrait Video that recreates the look of professional camera lenses. While the two features offer different camera experiences, both draw […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<blockquote class="wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p>Unveiled at Galaxy Unpacked July 2026, Samsung Electronics’ Galaxy Z series introduces My FanCam — a new feature that lets users focus their edits on a person of their choice — as well as an enhanced Portrait Video that recreates the look of professional camera lenses.<br><br>While the two features offer different camera experiences, both draw on expertise spanning foundational technology research, feature development, and user experience design. <br><br>As part of “The People Behind the Galaxy Z Series’ Camera Innovations,” Samsung Newsroom sat down with experts from Samsung Research and the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business to discuss the technical challenges and collaboration behind the two features.</p>
</blockquote>



<p>A sharply defined subject against a softly blurred background creates a sense of depth long associated with professional camera lenses. Researchers have continued to explore ways to achieve this effect in smartphone video.</p>



<p>With the Galaxy Z series, Portrait Video recreates the distinctive depth and Bokeh<sup data-fn="83cdb4ec-0894-4d23-a5eb-a5b37c5a501c" class="fn"><a href="#83cdb4ec-0894-4d23-a5eb-a5b37c5a501c" id="83cdb4ec-0894-4d23-a5eb-a5b37c5a501c-link">1</a></sup> of a large-aperture lens.<sup data-fn="8b03be77-63f5-4098-84b0-f9ff9f10c2ed" class="fn"><a href="#8b03be77-63f5-4098-84b0-f9ff9f10c2ed" id="8b03be77-63f5-4098-84b0-f9ff9f10c2ed-link">2</a></sup> To make this possible, Samsung Research analyzed the optical characteristics of actual camera lenses and developed AI-powered video processing technology, while the MX Business optimized it for the device.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="675" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16102150/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main1_F.jpg" alt="(From left) Sang-uk Park, responsible for camera development at the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business, and YoungGeol Kim of the Visual Technology Team at Samsung Research" class="wp-image-177329" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Sang-uk Park, responsible for camera development at the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business, and YoungGeol Kim of the Visual Technology Team at Samsung Research</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Newsroom spoke with YoungGeol Kim of the Visual Technology Team at Samsung Research and Sang-uk Park, responsible for camera development at the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business, about how Portrait Video evolved from a research concept into a Galaxy camera feature.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A New Level of Depth in Portrait Video</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q. What is Portrait Video? What has changed with the latest Galaxy Z series?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> Portrait Video keeps the main subject in focus while softly blurring the background, creating a cinematic look that makes the subject stand out.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="714" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15180950/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main2.jpg" alt="Users can open the Camera app and tap "More” to access Portrait Video." class="wp-image-177298" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Users can open the Camera app and tap “More” to access Portrait Video.</figcaption></figure>



<p>On the Galaxy Z series, the degree of blur changes naturally based on the distance between the subject and background. Small background lights appear as soft, rounded Bokeh, much as they would through a large-aperture lens. This allows users to draw attention to the subject and capture videos with greater depth and atmosphere using just a smartphone, without the need for separate professional equipment.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15182343/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main3.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Comparison of videos with and without Portrait Video on the Galaxy Z series</figcaption></figure>



<p>Users can also shift the focus to a different subject or adjust the blur level after filming.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16102236/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main4_F.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Users can change the subject in focus and adjust the blur level from 0 to 7.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What inspired the technology behind Portrait Video?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim: </strong>Photography has been my hobby for 25 years. That passion led me to camera technology research and development and sparked a lasting interest in the unique visual character of lenses.</p>



<p>As smartphone cameras advanced rapidly, photography benefited from a range of new features and improvements in image quality. Video evolved quickly as well, but I saw further potential in recreating the depth and background rendering of professional camera lenses. We proposed the idea during the research stage, just as the MX Business was exploring ways to help users create professional-looking videos more easily — paving the way for joint development.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="673" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16103141/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main5_F.jpg" alt="Kim shares the inspiration behind Portrait Video." class="wp-image-177334" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Kim shares the inspiration behind Portrait Video.</figcaption></figure>



<p><strong>Q. How did the teams collaborate to turn Portrait Video into a camera feature?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> Existing solutions alone weren’t enough to achieve the natural lens rendering we wanted. At the time, Samsung Research’s technology was still in the research stage, so applying it to a product was not an easy decision. Even so, the MX Business recognized its potential and decided to commercialize it.</p>



<p>From there, full-scale testing and refinement began. We evaluated the technology on actual devices and exchanged feedback, turning it into a camera feature for users.</p>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> To prepare the feature for real-world use, we had to shoot countless videos under a wide range of conditions and validate the results. Since the feature is centered on people, experts from both teams even served as models and filmed one another. We focused particularly on how naturally subjects were separated from their backgrounds and whether the blur and Bokeh looked right at different distances.</p>



<p>As we filmed each other, reviewed the results together and continued our discussions, what I remember most is how noticeably the results improved.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="693" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16103231/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main6_F.jpg" alt="Park describes the collaboration between the teams." class="wp-image-177335" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Park describes the collaboration between the teams.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Large-Aperture Lens Rendering on Smartphones</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q. What is the key technology that takes Portrait Video to the next level?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> The key is technology that recreates the distinctive look of large-aperture professional camera lenses in smartphone video. We used AI and optical analysis to make this possible.</p>



<p>Simply blurring an image is not enough to reproduce the depth and natural background rendering of a professional camera. Conventional blur softens an image by blending surrounding colors, but a large-aperture lens works differently. The degree of blur changes naturally based on the distance between the subject and background, while bright points of light spread into Bokeh that retain their shape and brightness. The shapes and textures of these out-of-focus areas are known as bokeh.</p>



<p>This requires accounting for both subject-to-background distance and how light spreads through an actual camera lens.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183133/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main7.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Bokeh captured with Portrait Video on Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra</figcaption></figure>



<p>Recreating this effect on a smartphone required two elements. First, we analyzed how light spreads through a real large-aperture lens and the resulting bokeh characteristics, then converted the results into data. We combined this with AI-estimated distance information for the scene, allowing the system to adjust the degree of blur and bokeh size based on the distance between the subject and background.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183206/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main8.jpg" alt="Process for recreating the effect of a large-aperture lens" class="wp-image-177306" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Process for recreating the effect of a large-aperture lens</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What technical challenges did the teams face when creating blur and bokeh on smartphones?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> The biggest challenge was naturally rendering complex boundaries between the subject and background, such as hair or eyeglass frames. On smartphones, AI-estimated distance information alone isn’t sufficient to accurately distinguish fine boundaries. Even a small error can blur strands of hair or leave parts of the background sharp, making the subject look unnaturally cut out.</p>



<p>To address this, we combined AI-estimated distance information with boundary information from the original footage, allowing for natural separation between the subject and background even around fine details such as hair.</p>



<p><strong>Park: </strong>Maintaining consistent results across different shooting conditions — including weather, lighting, subject movement, and distance — was crucial. We repeatedly filmed under a wide range of conditions and reviewed the results, improving unnatural elements through software optimization and additional AI training data. Building on Samsung Research’s technology, image quality experts from the MX Business also evaluated the results from a user perspective, further refining the feature.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183259/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main9.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Portrait Video recognizes faces and naturally shifts focus to another person when the main subject turns away.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What did it take to make this complex video processing reliable and ready for use on the Galaxy Z series?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim: </strong>The biggest hurdle was reducing power consumption and heat generation without compromising quality. Video is a rapid sequence of images, so processing 4K video in real time requires significant computation.</p>



<p>That meant we had to prioritize. We preserved the processing essential to image quality while reducing computation in areas where users were unlikely to notice a difference.</p>



<p>As a result, we struck a balance between natural lens rendering and real-time processing, ensuring stable, efficient performance even during extended recording.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>More Freedom in Video Creation</strong></h2>



<p><strong>Q.</strong> <strong>What are some tips to help users get the most out of Portrait Video?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> To make bokeh stand out, look for scenes with bright light sources against a relatively dark background. Sunlight sparkling through leaves, Christmas lights, or streetlights at night can spread softly into circular Bokeh, creating a more atmospheric look.</p>



<p>When filming a person, it’s best to avoid strong light from the front. Instead, position the light source behind or to the side of the subject. This can softly illuminate the subject’s hair and contours while making the background Bokeh more prominent. Reflected light or ambient sources like streetlights can help keep the face from getting too dark for a more balanced result.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183436/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main10.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ An object filmed at a café using Portrait Video. The naturally blurred background makes the subject stand out.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<p><strong>Q. What reaction would you most like to hear from people who have tried Portrait Video?</strong></p>



<p><strong>Park:</strong> I’d like users, creators, and video professionals to be surprised by how easy it is to create cinematic video on a smartphone. I hope the natural blur, Bokeh, and post-capture editing features give them greater freedom to achieve the look and feel they want.</p>



<p><strong>Kim:</strong> Ultimately, I hope people feel they no longer need to carry a separate professional camera. We will continue advancing the underlying technologies so they can create cinematic videos with just a smartphone.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="671" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/16102308/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main11_F.jpg" alt="The experts look forward to what’s next for Portrait Video." class="wp-image-177331" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The experts look forward to what’s next for Portrait Video.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Bringing the cinematic shallow depth of field and light rendering of professional camera lenses to smartphone video required extensive testing and collaboration to turn a research concept into a reliable product feature. Developed through the combined expertise of Samsung Research and the MX Business, Portrait Video expands the Galaxy video experience — giving users more ways to realize their creative vision.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="83cdb4ec-0894-4d23-a5eb-a5b37c5a501c">The shape and texture of blur in out-of-focus areas, often seen when small points of light appear as circular highlights. <a href="#83cdb4ec-0894-4d23-a5eb-a5b37c5a501c-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="8b03be77-63f5-4098-84b0-f9ff9f10c2ed">A lens with an aperture that can open wide to let in more light, making it well suited for keeping the subject sharp while softly blurring the background and rendering bokeh highlights. <a href="#8b03be77-63f5-4098-84b0-f9ff9f10c2ed-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

<enclosure url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183133/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main7.mp4" length="545" type="video/mp4" />
<enclosure url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183259/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main9.mp4" length="545" type="video/mp4" />
<enclosure url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/15183436/Samsung-Mobile-The-New-Galaxy-Z-series-Portrait-Video-Interview_main10.mp4" length="546" type="video/mp4" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung and Verizon Complete Successful ISAC Trial Over Virtualized Network at Global Sports Event]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-verizon-complete-successful-isac-trial-over-virtualized-network-at-global-sports-event</link>
				<pubDate>Mon, 14 Sep 2026 22:10:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/14091554/Samsung-Network-Solutions-Verizon-ISAC-Trial-Global-Sports-Event_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Network Solutions]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[6G]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[AI-powered virtualized RAN (vRAN)]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[ISAC]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Verizon]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4qTn1Wt</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and Verizon have successfully completed an Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) trial at a major international soccer fan event in Dallas, Texas, enabling real-time sensing-driven intelligence across a virtualized network. This landmark milestone represents one of the industry’s first practical implementations of a key technology for 6G, signaling how operators are leveraging software-driven […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics and Verizon have successfully completed an Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) trial at a major international soccer fan event in Dallas, Texas, enabling real-time sensing-driven intelligence across a virtualized network. This landmark milestone represents one of the industry’s first practical implementations of a key technology for 6G, signaling how operators are leveraging software-driven network architectures to create infrastructure that actively perceives, understands, and responds to their environment — moving beyond connectivity.</p>



<p>The groundbreaking field trial reinforces the value of the strategic partnership between Samsung and Verizon. Operating one of the world’s largest commercial virtualized networks, Verizon has led the charge in deploying a software-driven, AI-powered virtualized RAN (vRAN). This laid the foundation for a 6G-ready platform necessary to bring experimental use cases from concept to real-world validation — highlighting the flexibility of Samsung’s adaptable vRAN, which enables operators to introduce new capabilities like ISAC from day one by leveraging the commercial network they already run.</p>



<p>In the ISAC trial, Samsung integrated commercial AI-powered vRAN and vCore with an ISAC application, deploying them on its Network in a Server (NIS) — an end-to-end software-driven edge-AI platform that consolidates all network functions and accelerated compute into a single server. This allowed the ISAC trial to leverage connectivity from a 5G network without the need for any additional equipment.</p>



<p>Acting as a sensing receiver, a CBRS<sup data-fn="b64f551e-123c-4ab3-a5ae-c3f92cc48079" class="fn"><a href="#b64f551e-123c-4ab3-a5ae-c3f92cc48079" id="b64f551e-123c-4ab3-a5ae-c3f92cc48079-link">1</a></sup> radio unit using 40 MHz of spectrum mounted on a Verizon Cell-On-Wheels (COW) captured channel variations in transmissions from a commercial 5G device. Samsung’s NIS then analyzed this real-time data, utilizing the accelerated compute to extract actionable insights that are visualized as a crowd density heatmap that changes in near-real time to reflect people’s movements.<sup data-fn="df8165fb-fdbf-4357-ba6a-935fd28e11d3" class="fn"><a href="#df8165fb-fdbf-4357-ba6a-935fd28e11d3" id="df8165fb-fdbf-4357-ba6a-935fd28e11d3-link">2</a></sup></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/14092447/Samsung-Network-Solutions-Verizon-ISAC-Trial-Global-Sports-Event_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of the ISAC system" class="wp-image-177285" /></figure>



<p>Operating live at the event, the ISAC system used wireless signals as a sensing basis to automatically identify crowded areas within the venue, proving how sensing capabilities can enhance event management and the visitor experience across multiple stakeholder groups:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Attendees: </strong>Locate less congested food facilities and services, improving access and reducing wait times.</li>



<li><strong>Event Organizers: </strong>Gain real-time visibility into crowd distribution and facility usage, enabling rapid resource deployment and operational optimization.</li>



<li><strong>Security and Government Organizations:</strong> Detect emerging risks like overcrowding conditions, enabling early intervention to facilitate crowd control and prevent other public safety incidents.</li>
</ul>



<p>The achievement reflects the synergy between Samsung’s commercial technologies and its advanced research in accelerating the path to 6G. The company’s efforts include its commercially proven vRAN software on the edge-AI platform NIS, and Samsung Research America’s (SRA) cutting-edge AI-driven ISAC solution.</p>



<p>“Verizon’s strategy has always been focused on building an intelligent, AI-native network that translates into meaningful real-world value for our customers,” said Yago Tenorio, Chief Technology Officer and Senior Vice President of Technology Development at Verizon. “Our early investments in flexible, software-driven vRAN architecture are paying off today, giving us the agility to engineer and trial groundbreaking 6G concepts like ISAC over our existing infrastructure. This field trial with Samsung proves that the network of the future will do far more than just transfer data — it will actively perceive and interact with its environment to transform venue management and public safety.”</p>



<p>“This successful ISAC trial underscores how Verizon’s decision to adopt a software-driven, AI-powered vRAN early on laid a solid foundation, positioning the company at the forefront of the 6G journey,” said Ji-Yun Seol, Executive Vice President and Head of Product Strategy, Networks Business at Samsung Electronics. “With this approach, forward-thinking operators like Verizon are unlocking untapped potential, spearheading the shift toward a new era where unprecedented services, devices, and use cases converge on next-generation networks.”</p>



<p>“This ISAC trial marks a significant leap forward in integrating AI-driven sensing capabilities into network infrastructure, showcasing the transformative potential of 6G technologies,” said Charlie Zhang, Executive Vice President at Samsung Research America. “By combining Verizon’s innovative approach with Samsung’s advanced vRAN solutions, we’ve demonstrated the practical application of ISAC in a real-world scenario to enhance safety, efficiency, and user experience. This collaboration reinforces our commitment to pushing the boundaries of what’s possible in next-generation wireless networks.”</p>



<p>As a founding member of the <a href="https://www.verizon.com/about/news/verizon-leads-6g-momentum-expansion-isac-trials" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Verizon 6G Innovation Forum</a>, Samsung is exploring new use cases to advance innovation, research initiatives, and the overall charter of the consortium. The company continues to work alongside visionary partners to deliver transformative technologies and drive the transition to 6G.</p>



<p>Samsung Networks has pioneered the successful delivery of 5G end-to-end solutions, including chipsets, radios, and cores. Through ongoing research and development, Samsung is driving the industry to advance 5G networks and paving the way for 6G and beyond with its market-leading product portfolio, from purpose-built RAN, vRAN, Open RAN, AI RAN, and cloud-native core to private network solutions and AI-powered automation tools (CognitiV NOS). The company currently provides innovative network solutions to mobile operators that deliver boundless connectivity to hundreds of millions of users worldwide.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="b64f551e-123c-4ab3-a5ae-c3f92cc48079">Citizen Broadband Radio Service, a 150 MHz-wide band of the 3.5 GHz band in the United States. <a href="#b64f551e-123c-4ab3-a5ae-c3f92cc48079-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="df8165fb-fdbf-4357-ba6a-935fd28e11d3">The crowd sensing demo measures crowd density, which is the number of people in a given area, at any given time. No information is obtained about the identity of the individuals. <a href="#df8165fb-fdbf-4357-ba6a-935fd28e11d3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung and Mistral AI Announce Strategic Partnership for Intelligence-Driven Semiconductor Infrastructure]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-and-mistral-ai-announce-strategic-partnership-for-intelligence-driven-semiconductor-infrastructure</link>

								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08191740/Samsung-Semiconductors-Mistral-AI-Partnership_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />

The announcement of the partnership was made during the state summit held in Paris between South Korea and France. Under the agreement, Samsung will […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced it has entered into a strategic partnership with Mistral AI to enhance its semiconductor engineering and manufacturing capabilities that will reinforce its leadership in AI chip technologies.</p>



<p>The announcement of the partnership was made during the state summit held in Paris between South Korea and France. Under the agreement, Samsung will integrate Mistral’s AI services and solutions — including its flagship large language model, Mistral Large — across its semiconductor operations to develop customized on-premises AI models optimized for intelligence-driven infrastructure.</p>



<p>Mistral’s powerful AI platform is expected to provide Samsung with a unique stack of tools that will transform how semiconductors are designed and manufactured.</p>



<p>The on-premises enterprise solutions will especially ensure security and flexibility required to process highly sensitive technologies and operational data entirely within the boundaries of Samsung’s semiconductor infrastructure, maintaining total control over its mission-critical technologies.</p>



<p>As semiconductor processes become more advanced and complex, rapid data analysis in the fab is essential. By applying targeted AI models to defect detection and equipment optimization, Samsung aims to accelerate development cycles, manufacturing precision, and yield stabilization across its advanced memory and logic chips.</p>



<p>“Increasing complexities involved in AI chip design and manufacturing requires continuous innovation in semiconductor technologies,” said Young Hyun Jun, Vice Chairman & CEO of the Device Solutions (DS) Division at Samsung Electronics. “We look forward to working with Mistral and support the evolving needs of customers, while delivering new breakthroughs across the semiconductor ecosystem essential to the AI era.”</p>



<p>“AI is reshaping how we build complex technologies, from silicon to software,” said Arthur Mensch, co-founder and CEO of Mistral. “We are proud to support Samsung Electronics with our expertise in electronics and semiconductors, helping to improve how chips are designed and manufactured, and to accelerate technical progress across the global semiconductor and AI value chain.”</p>



<p>Samsung has also led Mistral AI’s Series D funding round, securing a strategic equity stake to support long-term technology collaboration. Samsung’s lead investment reflects expanding cross-industry collaboration across the advanced chip ecosystem.</p>



<p>By joining its leading silicon technologies with AI innovators, such as Mistral, Samsung plans to continue its progress toward a broader AI transformation across its semiconductor operations and meet the diverse needs of its customers spanning advanced memory and logic to foundry services.</p>

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[From 253 Grams to 201 Grams: The Engineering Behind the World’s Lightest Fold]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/from-253-grams-to-201-grams-the-engineering-behind-the-worlds-lightest-fold</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 09 Sep 2026 01:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08171612/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Foldable]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Hardware]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4iSJBwh</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Since introducing the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019, Samsung Electronics has continued to expand what a foldable smartphone can be, advancing hinge and display design, durability, and usability with every generation. As those technologies evolved, Samsung focused on another challenge: making a large-screen foldable easier to carry every day, without losing the […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Since introducing the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019, Samsung Electronics has continued to expand what a foldable smartphone can be, advancing hinge and display design, durability, and usability with every generation.</p>



<p>As those technologies evolved, Samsung focused on another challenge: making a large-screen foldable easier to carry every day, without losing the experiences that make the form factor unique.</p>



<p>And through that pursuit, Galaxy Z Fold5 weighed 253 grams, and Galaxy Z Fold6 reduced its weight further to 239 grams.</p>



<p>With Galaxy Z Fold7 came an even more ambitious goal:</p>



<p class="has-text-align-left">“Could a foldable be lighter than a bar-type flagship smartphone?”</p>



<p>At the time, Galaxy S25 Ultra — one of Samsung’s flagship bar-type smartphones — weighed 218 grams. So, the development team set an even lighter weight target for Galaxy Z Fold7: 215 grams.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold7 reached that goal. And now, building on years of foldable innovation, Galaxy Z Fold8 combines a new form factor with advances in core technologies, materials, and internal architecture to weigh just 201 grams — 52 grams lighter than Galaxy Z Fold5. At 201 grams, Galaxy Z Fold8 is the world’s lightest fold.<sup data-fn="b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d" class="fn"><a href="#b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d" id="b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d-link">1</a></sup></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08193810/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold’s weight and thickness by generation" class="wp-image-177277" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold’s weight and thickness by generation</figcaption></figure>



<p>But consumers expected more than just a lighter foldable.</p>



<p>A <a href="https://yougov.com/en-us/articles/50945-foldable-phones-high-interest-among-young-adults-durability-a-major-concern" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">2024 YouGov survey</a><sup data-fn="2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b" class="fn"><a href="#2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b" id="2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b-link">2</a></sup> found that a bulky or heavy design remained a concern among consumers not planning to purchase foldables, while battery life, durability, and display quality ranked as top priorities for those considering them.</p>



<p>This insight shaped a core engineering goal for Samsung: make foldables thinner and lighter, while maintaining the core experiences users value most.</p>



<p>Battery life, camera performance, thermal management, and durability still had to advance.</p>



<p>That could not be achieved by changing a single component. Samsung engineers had to reimagine the entire device — advancing fundamental foldable technologies, introducing new materials, and revisiting hundreds of components and auxiliary parts to the fraction-of-a-gram level.</p>



<p>From 253 grams to 201 grams. Three engineering innovations enabled that transformation.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08173710/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Main2.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ <em>From Galaxy Z Fold5 to Galaxy Z Fold8, Samsung reduced 52 grams to deliver the world’s lightest foldable.</em></figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">The Thinner and Lighter a Foldable Gets, the More Precise the Engineering Must Become</h2>



<p>Making a foldable thinner and lighter brings a new set of engineering challenges.</p>



<p>The layers inside the display need to become thinner while still meeting Samsung’s durability and reliability requirements. The metal plate supporting the display needs to stay extremely flat even as it becomes thinner. And the hinge has to manage the forces created every time the device folds and unfolds, while being designed around the size and structure of each product.</p>



<p>Across generations, Samsung has continued to advance the display layers, ultra-thin metal precision machining, and hinge technologies central to its foldable devices.</p>



<p>One example is <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-flex-titanium-technology-to-advance-foldable-displays" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Flex Titanium</a>.</p>



<p>The displays in Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra use Flex Titanium technology featuring a Titanium Alloy Film designed for the foldable display structure.</p>



<p>The film is just tens of micrometers thick — roughly one-third the width of a human hair. Its material and structure work together to maintain performance at this extreme thickness.</p>



<p>The metal plate that supports the display presents a different challenge.</p>



<p>Some metal plates are machined to approximately 0.2 millimeters thick, with the thinnest sections reaching 0.15 millimeters. At that level, simply making the metal thinner is not enough. Thin metal can bend or warp more easily during machining, and even a small change in shape can affect assembly precision or the surface quality of the display.</p>



<p>What matters is the ability to machine ultra-thin metal at around 0.2 millimeters while consistently achieving the required shape and flatness.</p>



<p>Samsung refined the machining sequence, tooling, cooling conditions, and process parameters, breaking the process into multiple stages to maintain precision.</p>



<p>The hinge requires the same level of care.</p>



<p>Beyond simply opening and closing the phone, it must distribute the forces created by repeated folding and unfolding across the device while maintaining structural stability.</p>



<p>And these requirements vary by product size and architecture.</p>



<p>Samsung engineers the hinge for each form factor, carefully balancing load distribution, strength, durability, internal space, and weight rather than just scaling it down from one generation to the next.</p>



<p>Creating a thinner and lighter foldable requires more than downsizing individual parts.</p>



<p>It comes from greater precision in how materials are machined, how forces are managed, and how structures are designed within a limited space.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Lighter, Built for Strength — Starting With the Materials</h2>



<p>The second area is materials innovation.</p>



<p>Different parts of a foldable have very different jobs. Some demand high rigidity. Some must withstand repeated movement. Others need to spread heat efficiently.</p>



<p>Samsung selects and develops materials around those specific needs, delivering the performance each requires while optimizing weight and space.</p>



<p>In Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Flex Titanium incorporates a Titanium Alloy Film to support the foldable display structure.</p>



<p>For the exterior, Samsung uses Advanced Armor Aluminum, helping provide the strength and durability needed in a thin and lightweight frame.</p>



<p>Materials also play an important role in thermal management.</p>



<p>As a device gets thinner, there is less room to manage the heat generated inside. Samsung uses graphite with improved thermal performance, while tailoring the overall thermal structure to the size and performance requirements of each product.</p>



<p>In Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, which has similar overall dimensions to Galaxy Z Fold7, the volume of the graphite heat-dissipation layer was increased by approximately 7% to help support its enhanced performance.</p>



<p>Samsung also applies Clad Metal inside the rear of the device. By combining the characteristics of different metals, the material helps reduce weight while providing the thermal performance required within a thin structure.</p>



<p>Materials innovation is therefore not just about choosing lighter options, but about using the right material in the right place to deliver the performance each part needs with less weight and space.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Looking Again at the Entire Device — Down to 0.001 Grams</h2>



<p>Only so much can be achieved through core technologies and new materials alone.</p>



<p>In a foldable already packed with highly integrated components, finding the next few grams means looking again at almost everything inside the device.</p>



<p>Samsung engineers reviewed approximately 270 components and more than 180 types of auxiliary parts.</p>



<p>This spanned everything from the display, hinge, and cameras to circuit boards, antennas, brackets, tapes, and fasteners.</p>



<p>The precision extended to reductions of 0.1 grams and 0.1 millimeters, and in some auxiliary parts, as little as 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>The printed circuit board, or PCB, was redesigned to remove unnecessary circuits, improve component layout, and minimize its footprint.</p>



<p>The MFC (Magnetic Flux Channel) antenna was also reimagined to reduce unnecessary overlap and interference with surrounding components, allowing the limited internal space to be used more efficiently.</p>



<p>Every auxiliary part, no matter how small, was examined.</p>



<p>Of the more than 180 types previously used, approximately 15 were eliminated.</p>



<p>Engineers then reviewed the remaining 170 types, trimming oversized areas, refining shapes, and optimizing dimensions. Multiple parts were consolidated into single structures where possible.</p>



<p>In some cases, the savings were measured in increments as small as 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>Each change seems minor in isolation, but repeated across the device, fractions of a gram become tenths of a gram, then whole grams.</p>



<p>The weight savings on a finished device result from hundreds of small engineering decisions.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Finding 6 Grams Again To Make Room for More</h2>



<p>The goal was to reinvest the weight and space recovered through engineering into the capabilities people actually experience — including the battery, cameras, and thermal system.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra provides a separate example of how this approach can be applied.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra and Galaxy Z Fold7 have similar overall dimensions and both weigh 215 grams. But within that same weight, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra increases battery capacity from 4,400 mAh to 5,000 mAh.</p>



<p>That larger battery brought its own challenge: the battery itself added approximately 6 grams.</p>



<p>So the development team faced a new question:</p>



<p class="has-text-align-left">“Where could we find those 6 grams again?”</p>



<p>There was no single component that could provide the answer.</p>



<p>Instead, engineers applied the same systematic approach across the entire device — materials and structures, circuit layouts, and component placement, down to the smallest auxiliary parts — identifying weight savings across all components.</p>



<p>This strategy extended to internal space optimization.</p>



<p>By reducing unnecessary overlap between the display, circuits, antennas, and surrounding components, and by improving packaging efficiency, Samsung created room for a larger battery and enhanced thermal and camera systems.</p>



<p>As a result, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra accommodates a 5,000 mAh battery while increasing the volume of its graphite heat-dissipation layer by approximately 7% compared with the similarly sized Galaxy Z Fold7.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a 200 MP main camera and introduces the first 50 MP Ultra-Wide camera on a Galaxy Z Fold device.</p>



<p>Samsung’s approach to a thinner and lighter foldable is therefore not about removing capabilities.</p>



<p>It is about redirecting weight and space savings toward the flagship experiences users value most.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">From 253 Grams to 201 Grams: Redefining the Weight Standard for Foldables</h2>



<p>The journey began with the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019. Across eight generations, Samsung has continued to advance the technologies, materials, and internal architecture behind the foldable experience.</p>



<p>In recent generations, that progress accelerated — from 253 grams with Galaxy Z Fold5 to 201 grams with Galaxy Z Fold8.</p>



<p>But the story isn’t just about reducing weight.</p>



<p>Samsung’s goal has been to make a large-screen foldable easier to carry without giving up the qualities users expect from a flagship smartphone.</p>



<p>The weight and space recovered through advances in core technologies, materials, and component design were put back into the battery, cameras, thermal system, and other flagship experiences users value most.</p>



<p>From 253 grams to 201 grams.</p>



<p>No single material or component made that transformation possible.</p>



<p>Eight generations of engineering enabled this, advancing core technologies, introducing new materials, and rethinking the entire device to make Galaxy Z Fold easier to carry without compromising the flagship experience.</p>



<p><strong><strong>Galaxy Z Fold Series: A Thinner, Lighter Journey in Numbers</strong></strong></p>



<p>– 52 g<br>Galaxy Z Fold8 is 52 grams lighter than Galaxy Z Fold5.</p>



<p>1/3, 0.2 mm<br>The Titanium Alloy Film used in Flex Titanium is approximately one-third the width of a human hair, while ultra-thin metal structures supporting the display are precision-machined to around 0.2 millimeters.</p>



<p>~270 / 180+ / 0.001 g<br>Samsung re-examined approximately 270 components and more than 180 types of auxiliary parts, optimizing some down to 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>5,000 mAh / +~7% / 200 MP + 50 MP<br>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a 5,000 mAh battery, approximately 7% more graphite heat-dissipation volume and a 200 MP main camera with a 50 MP Ultra-Wide camera.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d">Based on internal research as of July 22, 2026, comparing commercially available book-style foldable smartphones globally. Exact weight may vary by model, configuration, and manufacturing process. Weight measured excluding protective cases or accessories. <a href="#b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b">Source: YouGov plc, 2024, © All rights reserved <a href="#2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>
													<enclosure url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08173710/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Main2.mp4" length="500" type="video/mp4" />

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and ASML Expand Strategic Collaboration for Next-Generation Semiconductor Manufacturing]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-and-asml-expand-strategic-collaboration-for-next-generation-semiconductor-manufacturing</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 08 Sep 2026 15:03:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08110616/Samsung-Semiconductors-ASML-Partnership_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Semiconductors]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[ASML]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[DRAM]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[EUV lithography technology]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4iRCkgc</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and ASML today announced an expansion of their long-standing strategic partnership, deepening collaboration on High NA EUV lithography and advanced semiconductor manufacturing technologies that will help drive the next generation of innovation in the AI era. Building on years of successful cooperation, the companies will work together to accelerate the development and deployment […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics and ASML today announced an expansion of their long-standing strategic partnership, deepening collaboration on High NA EUV lithography and advanced semiconductor manufacturing technologies that will help drive the next generation of innovation in the AI era.</p>



<p>Building on years of successful cooperation, the companies will work together to accelerate the development and deployment of technologies that support the increasing performance and efficiency requirements of advanced semiconductor manufacturing.</p>



<p>Samsung will join the industry initiative to advance next-generation 12-inch photomask technology for future manufacturing. The semiconductor industry has relied on the 6-inch photomask format for decades. With High NA EUV, the transition to larger, 12-inch masks is expected to further increase fab productivity, lower chipmaking costs and remove stitching constraints. It will enable advanced chip manufacturers to fully leverage the advantages of High NA EUV, making future generations of leading-edge chips more cost-effective.</p>



<p>Drawing on its leadership in both memory and foundry manufacturing, as well as its extensive experience with advanced EUV lithography, Samsung is positioned to play a critical role in the 12-inch photomask transition. The company will work closely with industry partners to develop the required mask technologies and supporting infrastructure.</p>



<p>Samsung also plans to introduce ASML’s High NA extreme ultraviolet (EUV) lithography into future DRAM high-volume manufacturing for the first time in the industry by 2028, further demonstrating the readiness of High NA technology to support advanced memory and logic applications. High NA EUV is expected to extend the DRAM scaling roadmap, with improved resolution enabling process simplification and increased efficiency.</p>



<p>The announcement reflects a shared commitment to innovation, technology leadership, and long-term partnership. Samsung and ASML expect to continue exploring opportunities for collaboration in areas including advanced memory and innovative manufacturing approaches.</p>



<p>Young Hyun Jun, Vice Chairman & CEO, Samsung Electronics, said: “The AI era is transforming the semiconductor industry and increasing the importance of technological innovation across the entire value chain. Samsung is committed to maintaining leadership in advanced semiconductor manufacturing, including memory and foundry, through breakthrough technologies and strong partnerships. By further strengthening our collaboration with ASML, we are helping lay the foundation for the next generation of AI and semiconductor innovation.”</p>



<p>Christophe Fouquet, President and Chief Executive Officer, ASML, said: “Samsung has been one of ASML’s most important innovation partners for many years. Together, we have helped advance the technologies that underpin modern semiconductor manufacturing. As the industry enters a new era driven by artificial intelligence, close collaboration with our customers becomes even more important. We look forward to working with Samsung to enable the next wave of semiconductor innovation.”</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>About ASML Holding N.V.</strong><br>ASML is a leading supplier to the semiconductor industry. The company provides chipmakers with hardware, software and services to mass produce the patterns of integrated circuits (microchips). Together with its partners, ASML drives the advancement of more affordable, more powerful, more energy-efficient microchips. ASML enables groundbreaking technology to solve some of humanity’s toughest challenges, such as in healthcare, energy use and conservation, mobility, and agriculture. ASML is a multinational company headquartered in Veldhoven, the Netherlands, with offices across EMEA, the US and Asia. Every day, ASML’s more than 44,000 employees (FTE) challenge the status quo and push technology to new limits. ASML is traded on Euronext Amsterdam and NASDAQ under the symbol ASML. Discover ASML — our products, technology and career opportunities — at <a href="https://www.asml.com/en/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">www.asml.com</a>.</h4>

				<title><![CDATA[Immaculate Engineering Meets Artistic Imagination: Highlights From Design Miami Seoul 2026]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/immaculate-engineering-meets-artistic-imagination-highlights-from-design-miami-seoul-2026</link>

									<description><![CDATA[What happens when human imagination, artificial intelligence, art and technology come together?  From September 1 to 6, Samsung Electronics presented a special exhibition themed “Design is an Act of Love” during Design Miami Seoul 2026 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul. The exhibition offered a fresh interpretation of Samsung’s human-centered design vision. Inspired by […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>What happens when human imagination, artificial intelligence, art and technology come together?  From September 1 to 6, Samsung Electronics presented a special exhibition themed “Design is an Act of Love” during Design Miami Seoul 2026 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul.</p>



<p>The exhibition offered a fresh interpretation of Samsung’s human-centered design vision. Inspired by Samsung products, 14 Korean artists drew on their sensibilities and imagination to interpret an act of love in their own ways. In the process, some used AI to develop their ideas and expand the possibilities of creative expression. Visitors viewed the completed works on OLED TV S95H displays, each featuring a custom frame with distinct materials and forms.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom visited “Design is an Act of Love” to see how art, technology, and design came together.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="580" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07102339/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main1.jpg" alt="Visitors explore Samsung’s exhibition at Design Miami Seoul 2026." class="wp-image-177126" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors explore Samsung’s exhibition at Design Miami Seoul 2026.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>14 Acts of Love Inspired by Samsung Products</strong></h2>



<p>Upon entering, visitors were greeted by 14 artworks displayed on OLED TV S95H screens. Moving through the space, they encountered distinct colors, compositions, and narratives — each offering a different interpretation of a Samsung product.</p>



<p>Beyond the individual artworks, the exhibition explored the evolving relationship between human creativity and artificial intelligence, expressed through the formula AI × (EI + HI) — Artificial Intelligence amplified by Emotional Intelligence and Human Imagination.</p>



<p>The artworks began with human intuition, emotion, point of view, and imagination. Some artists then entered into a dialogue with AI, using it not to replace their creativity, but to expand it, animate it, and take it into unexpected new territories. Technology amplifies the human. It does not replace it.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="509" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07102438/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main2.jpg" alt="The works were inspired by 14 Samsung products spanning mobile devices, TVs, and home appliances." class="wp-image-177127" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The works were inspired by 14 Samsung products spanning mobile devices, TVs, and home appliances.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Aihyung Jo’s “EVERYTHING, SO FAR…” draws inspiration from Galaxy Z Fold8, using metaphor and humor to explore the human desire to express one’s feelings to others.</p>



<p>Across the foldable’s inner and outer displays, visual symbols of human progress illustrate how technology has expanded human capabilities and opened countless possibilities.</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ A visitor takes in “EVERYTHING, SO FAR…” by Aihyung Jo.</h4>



<p>“The Shape of Sound” by design studio Everyday Practice reinterprets the exhibition theme as an act of connecting people.</p>



<p>Inspired by Music Studio 5, the work visualizes sound not simply as something to be heard but as a shared experience. Waveforms radiating from the center and expanding layers show how a single sound fills a space, bringing together those within it.</p>

<p>In “Gitdeun,” kkamangdol reimagines the Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub as a reflection of a family’s tastes and memories, capturing an everyday act of love.</p>



<p>Drawing on the tenderness found in familiar things, the work evokes the warmth and anticipation of February as winter gives way to spring.</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ A visitor looks at “Gitdeun” by kkamangdol.</h4>



<p>“The Aural Sensation” by Sukeee connects the immersive musical experience and sensory resonance of Galaxy Buds4 Pro with the vitality of nature.</p>



<p>An earbud sits at the center of an organic, ear-shaped flower, surrounded by rippling sound waves and fluttering petals that bring the scene to life.</p>



<p>“I wanted to capture an experience in which technology gently embraces the user’s emotions,” said Sukeee. “After developing the initial concept, I used generative AI tools throughout the process, from sketching to the final piece.”</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ Sukeee discusses the inspiration behind “The Aural Sensation.”</h4>



<p>While their approaches differed, human experience and emotion remained at the heart of each work — with some artists incorporating AI into their creative process.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>One Display, 14 Distinct Expressions</strong></h2>



<p>A key part of the showcase was the custom frames surrounding the artworks. Rather than following a single style, the 14 frames created for OLED TV S95H featured distinct materials, forms, and finishes. Each gave the same display a different character.</p>



<p>The frames also reflect the exhibition’s exploration of expression. After years in which technology has increasingly gravitated toward neutral, minimal, and universal forms, the exhibition explored a different possibility: bringing greater diversity, personality, energy, and emotion into the objects that surround us, and allowing technology to connect more deeply with individual identity.</p>



<p>For “Spring Carries Love,” Guimi Yoo reimagines The Frame Pro as a bridge between viewers and the world within the artwork. The piece was presented in a mint-colored acrylic frame with a hazy finish.</p>

<p>Inspired by the Infinite AI Air Purifier, “Picnic of May” by design studio Kiljong Arcade depicts a peaceful everyday moment. The work was displayed within a blue lattice frame combining matte and glossy finishes.</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ “Picnic of May” by Kiljong Arcade</h4>



<p>“Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory” presents Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 as companions that document users’ daily lives.</p>



<p>“I visualized the various types of everyday data accumulated on Galaxy Watch through changes in typography, color, lines, and density,” said Byungrok Chae, the artist behind “Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory.” “I extended the pixel structure of the digital display into the custom frame so the screen and frame flow together as a single graphic. This helped bring out the meaning and visual characteristics of Galaxy Watch.”</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ Chae explains the concept behind “Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory.”</h4>



<p>Samsung’s OLED TV S95H became a canvas for this exploration. Its Float Layer Design makes the display appear to float above a real metal plate, and for Design Miami Seoul, this concept was taken further through 14 unique custom frames, each created in dialogue with the artwork displayed on screen.</p>



<p>By incorporating its structure and materials as design elements, the TV itself becomes a design object within the space. Paired with 14 custom frames, the display showed how the same product could take on different expressions depending on its surroundings and individual tastes, bringing Samsung’s Expressive Design philosophy to life.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Human-Centered Design in the AI Era</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="680" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07144911/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main17.jpg" alt="Porcini at Design Miami Seoul 2026" class="wp-image-177170" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Porcini at Design Miami Seoul 2026</figcaption></figure>



<p>During the exhibition, Samsung hosted a design seminar titled “The Language of Creativity,” where Mauro Porcini, President and Chief Design Officer (CDO) of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics, discussed the company’s human-centered design vision and the role of design in the AI era.</p>



<p>Porcini emphasized that technology should bring warmth and value to people’s lives, while AI can expand human empathy and imagination.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="719" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07144931/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main18.jpg" alt="Porcini shares Samsung’s design vision." class="wp-image-177171" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Porcini shares Samsung’s design vision.</figcaption></figure>



<p>“This exhibition stems from our belief that ‘Design is an Act of Love,’” said Porcini. “Through it, we hope to show how technology can enrich what makes us human.”</p>



<p>Together, these ideas offered a broader interpretation of “Design is an Act of Love”: understanding people deeply enough to celebrate their differences, amplify their imagination, and use technology to create more human, diverse, and expressive possibilities.</p>



<p>At Design Miami Seoul 2026, Samsung demonstrated how its human-centered design vision extends across products and spaces when paired with artists’ imaginations. As the AI era continues to unfold, the company remains focused on design that understands and cares for people.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Medison Expands Women’s Health Diagnostic Portfolio With Launch of Premium Ultrasound ‘HERA Z10’]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-medison-expands-womens-health-diagnostic-portfolio-with-launch-of-premium-ultrasound-hera-z10</link>
				<pubDate>Mon, 07 Sep 2026 09:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07082901/Samsung-More-Products-Samsung-Medison-HERA-Z10_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[More Products]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[HERA Z Series]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[HERA Z10]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[ISUOG]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Medison]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Ultrasound System]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4yiYYCL</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Medison launched its premium obstetrics and gynecology (OB/GYN) ultrasound diagnostic system, the HERA Z10. Alongside the flagship HERA Z20, the new system officially completes the HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup. With global trends showing an increase in maternal age and pregnancy-related complications, precision imaging for prenatal diagnostics and rapid clinical decision-making have […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Medison launched its premium obstetrics and gynecology (OB/GYN) ultrasound diagnostic system, the HERA Z10. Alongside the flagship HERA Z20, the new system officially completes the HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="700" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07082959/Samsung-More-Products-Samsung-Medison-HERA-Z10_Main1.jpg" alt=" HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup" class="wp-image-177116" /></figure>



<p>With global trends showing an increase in maternal age and pregnancy-related complications, precision imaging for prenatal diagnostics and rapid clinical decision-making have become increasingly vital. As a result, women’s health diagnostic systems are evolving beyond basic fetal evaluations to encompass early complication prediction, maternal health assessments, and holistic wellness.</p>



<p>Aligning with these global clinical needs, Samsung Medison showcased the HERA Z Series at the 34th International Society of Ultrasound in Obstetrics and Gynecology (ISUOG) World Congress held in London from September 4 to 7.</p>



<p>Under the banner “HERA Z, The Flagship Series,” Samsung Medison presented its vision for next-generation diagnostic solutions, showcasing the series as a unified platform that spans the full spectrum of women’s healthcare.</p>



<p>The HERA Z Series serves as a comprehensive diagnostic platform designed to support maternal and fetal care alike, featuring AI-assisted automated measurement tools and dedicated neonatal probes.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Advanced AI Workflows and Next-Generation Imaging Architecture</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="686" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07083053/Samsung-More-Products-Samsung-Medison-HERA-Z10_Main2.jpg" alt="An image of HERA Z10" class="wp-image-177117" /></figure>



<p>The newly launched HERA Z10 inherits the proven AI-based workflow solutions of the flagship HERA Z20 while incorporating advanced beamforming technology designed to maximize image clarity. This expanded lineup empowers healthcare institutions to choose from a more versatile selection of premium systems within the HERA Z Series, perfectly tailored to their unique clinical environments and operational goals.</p>



<p>Engineered specifically to streamline clinical workflows through artificial intelligence, the HERA Z10 delivers significant efficiency gains:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Live ViewAssist<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: According to joint clinical research conducted with Professor Giuseppe Rizzo and Professor Daniele Di Mascio of Sapienza University of Rome — founding members of ISUOG — Live ViewAssist<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> reduces scan times during mid-trimester detailed ultrasound examinations by up to 52%.</li>



<li><strong>EzStructure<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Reduces manual manipulation for key measurement parameters by up to 71%, significantly enhancing measurement consistency and objectivity while dramatically lowering clinician fatigue.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Enhanced AI Diagnostic Assistance for High-Risk Pregnancies</strong></h2>



<p>The system also introduces enhanced AI-assisted diagnostic capabilities for complex clinical scenarios:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>MV-Flow 3D<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Captures microvascular blood flow at high resolution, providing critical clinical value for managing high-risk pregnancies, aiding in the diagnosis of placenta accreta and evaluation of  abnormal uterine or umbilical cord blood vessels.</li>



<li><strong>FibroidVue<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Utilizes AI to automatically segment, visualize, and measure the uterus, endometrium, and uterine fibroids, facilitating intuitive and efficient clinical assessments.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Interactive Experiences and Digital Synergy at ISUOG</strong></h2>



<p>At the ISUOG Congress, Samsung Medison hosted interactive experience zones highlighting the core value proposition of the HERA Z Series. Under the theme “ART OF ULTRASOUND,” an AI digital art exhibition reinterpreting fetal ultrasound images drew strong interest from attendees. Additionally, Samsung Medison demonstrated an integrated digital solution using Galaxy Tab devices, allowing clinicians and mothers to seamlessly monitor fetal growth records throughout the pregnancy journey.</p>



<p>“The launch of the HERA Z10 is more than just an addition to our product lineup; it represents a major milestone in extending the distinctive clinical value of the HERA Z Series to a wider range of healthcare settings,” said Jihyun Kim, Head of CX Team at Samsung Medison. “By leveraging premium imaging technology and unparalleled AI workflows, we remain committed to meeting diverse clinical needs and driving brand innovation so that healthcare professionals worldwide can select custom-fit solutions optimized for their unique practice environments.”</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Enhances Long-Term Value of Refrigerators and Laundry Appliances With AI-Focused Software Updates]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-enhances-long-term-value-of-refrigerators-and-laundry-appliances-with-ai-focused-software-updates</link>

								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/04103519/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Long-Term-Value-Software-Updates_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced major software updates for select refrigerators and laundry appliances. Beginning in September, the update to Tizen OS 10.0 will bring key services and features to select existing appliances in line with those offered on models launched this year. “Home appliances are products that consumers rely on for many years. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/04103543/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Long-Term-Value-Software-Updates_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics today announced major software updates for select refrigerators and laundry appliances." class="wp-image-177098" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced major software updates<sup data-fn="bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f" class="fn"><a href="#bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f" id="bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f-link">1</a></sup> for select refrigerators and laundry appliances. Beginning in September, the update to Tizen OS 10.0 will bring key services and features to select existing appliances in line with those offered on models launched this year.</p>



<p>“Home appliances are products that consumers rely on for many years. We believe supporting them over time requires not only reliable hardware, but also software that continues to evolve,” said Jeong Seung Moon, Executive Vice President and Head of the R&D Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Through this latest regular software update, we aim to help our appliances remain useful and up to date throughout the ownership journey, enhancing the long-term value consumers receive from products they already own.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub’s Newest AI Features Expand Globally</strong></h2>



<p>Building on the software update first introduced to <a href="https://bit.ly/3QTaNiD" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">select Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hubs in the U.S. in May</a>, Samsung is expanding its newest AI-powered refrigerator experiences to additional global markets.</p>



<p>Refrigerators with built-in cameras and AI Vision, including 21.5-inch and 32-inch Family Hub released between 2021 and 2025 — as well as select 9-inch Family Hub models released in 2025 — will receive the updated AI Vision built with Google Gemini.<sup data-fn="e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070" class="fn"><a href="#e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070" id="e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070-link">2</a></sup> The update expands the refrigerator’s ability to recognize and manage food items, helping users keep track of what is stored inside and make everyday food management easier.</p>



<p>With this update, the refrigerator can recognize a much wider range of fresh and packaged foods, including branded and region-specific items.</p>



<p>In addition, screen-based experiences like Now Brief<sup data-fn="adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88" class="fn"><a href="#adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88" id="adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88-link">3</a></sup> and What’s for Today? provide users with personalized information and help simplify meal decisions on a daily basis.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Seamless Interaction With Bespoke AI Laundry Appliances</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung is also extending its latest software experiences to select laundry appliances released in 2024 and 2025, including Bespoke AI Laundry Combo, Washer, and Dryer models.</p>



<p>A key part of the update is Bixby,<sup data-fn="64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15" class="fn"><a href="#64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15" id="64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15-link">4</a></sup> which enhances voice recognition and enables more natural interactions with laundry appliances. This allows users to communicate with their appliances more intuitively while carrying out everyday laundry tasks. For example, users can simply say, “When will my laundry be finished?” and Bixby will understand and reply with a phrase such as, “Your laundry is set to finish in two hours and 25 minutes.”<sup data-fn="d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7" class="fn"><a href="#d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7" id="d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7-link">5</a></sup></p>



<p>The update will introduce a refreshed interface aligned with Samsung’s latest laundry experience, offering more intuitive controls and information delivery on applicable models equipped with 7-inch Smart Screens. This will be achieved by adding Easy Mode,<sup data-fn="753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465" class="fn"><a href="#753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465" id="753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465-link">6</a></sup> which streamlines the screen layout and simplifies key information in an easy-to-access format.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>The updates will begin rolling out in phases from September 2026. Actual timing, supported features, and availability may vary by product, model year, and market.<sup data-fn="beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78" class="fn"><a href="#beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78" id="beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78-link">7</a></sup></p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Wins Multiple Honors at IFA Innovation Awards 2026]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-wins-multiple-honors-at-ifa-innovation-awards-2026</link>
				<pubDate>Fri, 04 Sep 2026 21:30:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03111725/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-IFA-Innovation-Awards-2026-Bespoke-AI-WindFree-Pro-and-Refrigerators_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />

						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>

		<category><![CDATA[Bespoke AI Refrigerator]]></category>
